<script>
    function initDfpScript() {
        var dfpConfig = {};
      if (dfpConfig) {
          window.q = new DFP.default(dfpConfig);
          window.q.initGoogleTag()
                  .then(function googleTagReady() {
                      if (typeof AdBlockUtil !== 'undefined' && typeof AdBlockUtil.killadblock === "function") {
                          window.addEventListener('adblockDetected', AdBlockUtil.killadblock);
                      }
                      googletag.pubads().collapseEmptyDivs();
                      if(typeof q.adManager.showAllSlots === 'function'){
                          q.adManager.showAllSlots();
                      }
                  })
      }
      else {
          throw new Error("Init Part I: dfpConfig is not ready!");
      }
        if (dfpConfig) {
          window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function loadAllAds() {
              window.q.initGoogleTag().then(function googleTagReady() {
				  if(typeof q.adManager.showAllSlots === 'function'){
                      q.adManager.showAllSlots();
				  }
              })
          })
      }
      else {
          throw new Error("Init Part II: dfpConfig is not ready!");
      }
        if (dfpConfig) {
          window.addEventListener('load', function loadAllAds() {
              setTimeout(function() {
                  window.q.initGoogleTag().then(function googleTagReady() {
                    if(typeof q.adManager.showAllSlots === 'function') {
						q.adManager.showAllSlots();
					}
                  });
              }, 10000);
          });
      }
      else {
          throw new Error("Init Part III: dfpConfig is not ready!");
      }
  }
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
function getCookie(){
	var cookieName = arguments[0];
	var cookieValue = null;
	if (document.cookie && document.cookie != '') {
		var cookies = document.cookie.split(';');
		for (var i = 0; i < cookies.length; i++) {
			var cookie = (cookies[i]).trim();			
			// Does this cookie string begin with the name we want?
			if (cookie.substring(0, cookieName.length + 1) == (cookieName + '=')) {
				cookieValue = unescape(cookie.substring(cookieName.length + 1));
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	return cookieValue;
}

    fv.appendToErrorMessages("userName", "empty", "Error: Enter Email");
    fv.appendToErrorMessages("userName", "not-valid", "Error: Enter valid Email");
    fv.appendToErrorMessages("password", "not-valid", "Please enter a password of six characters or more");
    fv.appendToErrorMessages("confirmPassword", "no-match", "Error: Passwords do not match");

                                        theme:			'owl--fullscreen',
                                        title:			'fullscreen slideshow: Israel Election, 2019 - in Pictures',
                                        navigation : 	true, // Show next and prev buttons
                                        pagination:		false,
                                        slideSpeed : 	1000,
                                        autoPlay: 		false,
                                        navigationText: ['<span  class="btn--icn--d"><svg class="icn  icn--b icn--middle" aria-label="Previous slide"><use xlink:href="#icon-prev" /></svg></span>',
                                                '<span  class="btn--icn--d"><svg class="icn  icn--b icn--middle" aria-label="Next slide"><use xlink:href="#icon-next" /></svg></span>'],
                                        paginationSpeed : 400,

                                        singleItem:		true,
                                        afterInit: 		function (el){
                                                fsIdx = gallery__1_7855689.fullscreen.find('.owl-item [data-img-id="' + imgId +'"]').parent().index();

						gallery__1_7855689.fullscreen.trigger("owl.jumpTo", fsIdx );

                                                if(gallery__1_7855689.data.info){
                                                        fsGallery.find('.js-gallery--facebook').click(function(){
                                                                window.open('https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?&u=' + encodeURIComponent(gallery__1_7855689.data.info.path) ,'popup','width=635,height=342,scrollbars=no,resizable=no,toolbar=no,directories=no,location=no,menubar=no,status=no');
                                                        });
                                                        fsGallery.find('.js-gallery--twitter').click(function(){
                                                                window.open('https://twitter.com/share?text=' + encodeURIComponent(gallery__1_7855689.data.info.text) + '&url=' + encodeURIComponent(gallery__1_7855689.data.info.path) ,'popup','width=635,height=342,scrollbars=no,resizable=no,toolbar=no,directories=no,location=no,menubar=no,status=no');
                                                        });
                                                }
                                        }
                                });

                        }else{
                                fsIdx = gallery__1_7855689.fullscreen.find('.owl-item [data-img-id="' + imgId +'"]').parent().index();
				gallery__1_7855689.fullscreen.trigger("owl.jumpTo", fsIdx );
                        }

                        $('#gallery__1_7855689_modal-full-screen-gallery').showDialog( { 'type' : 'modal' , 'onShowFocus' : gallery__1_7855689.fullscreen , 'onHideFocus' : this } );
                });
        });

        var OwlSync = {
                sync : function(gallery1, gallery2){
                        var ctrl1 = gallery1.data('owlCarousel'),
                                ctrl2 = gallery2.data('owlCarousel');

                        if(ctrl1 && ctrl2){
                                current = ctrl1.currentItem;

                                gallery2.find(".owl-item")
                                        .removeClass("synced")
                                        .eq(current)
                                        .addClass("synced");

                                this.center(gallery2, current);
                        }
                },

                center : function(gallery, number){
                        var galleryvisible = gallery.data("owlCarousel").owl.visibleItems,
                                num = number,
                                found = ( galleryvisible.indexOf(num) > -1 );

                        if(found===false){
                                if(num > galleryvisible[galleryvisible.length-1]){
                                        gallery.trigger("owl.goTo", num - galleryvisible.length+2)
                                }else{
                                        gallery.trigger("owl.goTo", num);
                                }
                        } else if(num === galleryvisible[galleryvisible.length-1]){
                                gallery.trigger("owl.goTo", galleryvisible[1])
                        } else if(num === galleryvisible[0]){
                                gallery.trigger("owl.goTo", num-1)
                        }

                        $(".imgCredit").text(window['gallery__1_7855689'].data.images[num].caption + ", " +  window['gallery__1_7855689'].data.images[num].credit);

                }
        }
</script>
</div>
</div>

      	    init: function() {
                base = this;
                if (UserUtil.getLoginInfo().userType != 'paying') {
                    $(".close--ruler").click(function() {
                      Ruler.collapsed();
                    });
                    $(".close--ruler").hover(function() {
                      $(".close--ruler").css('transform', 'rotate(180deg)').css('transition', 'transform 1s');
                    });
                    rulerDiv.show();
                    Ruler.expanded();
                    setTimeout(Ruler.collapsed, parseInt(Ruler.settings.timeout));
				}else{
          			var event = new Event('noRulerDetected');
					window.dispatchEvent(event);
        		}
            },
            expanded: function() {
                (rulerDiv[0]).style.height = "150px";
                (expandedDiv[0]).style.height = "150px";
                expandedDiv.html('<a data-stat-action="3" data-stat-campaign-name="Understand the middle east" data-stat-campaign-details="Subscribe" data-stat-feature="bottom strip subscribe" data-stat-feature-type="Marketing" href="'+Ruler.settings.link.replace("pubads.g.doubleclick.net","rmkz.themarker.com") +'"><img alt = "promotional content" title = "לרכישת מינוי הארץ" src="' + Ruler.settings.expandedImg + '"/></a>');
            },
            collapsed: function() {
                (expandedDiv[0]).style.height = "0px";
                (collapsedDiv[0]).style.height = "60px";
                (rulerDiv[0]).style.height = "60px";
                $(".close--ruler").hide();
                collapsedDiv.html('<a data-stat-action="3" data-stat-campaign-name="Understand the middle east" data-stat-campaign-details="Subscribe" data-stat-feature="bottom strip subscribe" data-stat-feature-type="Marketing" href="'+Ruler.settings.link.replace("pubads.g.doubleclick.net","rmkz.themarker.com") +'"><img alt = "promotional content" title = "לרכישת מינוי הארץ" src="' + Ruler.settings.collapsedImg + '"/></a>');
            },
			shouldbeShownToday: function() {
				var today = (new Date());
				today.setHours(0);
                today.setMinutes(0);
                today.setSeconds(0);
				if (!localStorage.dayInterval){
					var dateTimeInterval = [];
					var dayInterval = Ruler.settings.interval.split(",");	
					$.each(dayInterval, function( index, value ) {
						var nextIntervalDay = new Date();
						nextIntervalDay.setHours(0);
						nextIntervalDay.setMinutes(0);
						nextIntervalDay.setSeconds(0);
						var nextDay = (nextIntervalDay).setDate(today.getDate() + parseInt(value));
						dateTimeInterval.push(nextDay);
					});
					localStorage.dayInterval = JSON.stringify(dateTimeInterval); 
					return true;
				} else {
					var dateTimeInterval =  JSON.parse(localStorage.dayInterval)
					if (dateTimeInterval.length > 0){
						var nextDateTimeInterval = dateTimeInterval[0];
						if (nextDateTimeInterval <= today){
							dateTimeInterval.shift();
							localStorage.dayInterval = JSON.stringify(dateTimeInterval);
							return true;
						}
					} else{
						var nextIntervalDay = new Date();
						nextIntervalDay.setHours(0);
						nextIntervalDay.setMinutes(0);
						nextIntervalDay.setSeconds(0);
						dateTimeInterval.push((nextIntervalDay).setDate(today.getDate() + 1));
						localStorage.dayInterval = JSON.stringify(dateTimeInterval);
						return false;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}
		}
		window.addEventListener('noPopupDetected', Ruler.init);
    })();
</script>
